are defined in (
B.30
) and (
B.24
) respectively. We develop similar upper bounds for
\beta_{\textup{N}}^{(j+1)}
and
\mathbf{V}^{(j+1)}
. Combining these upper bounds, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j+1)}\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}+\eta^{2}\left(Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},2}^{(j)}+Q_{\beta_{\textup{N}},2}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{U},2}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{V},2}^{(j)}\right)

(B.5)

\displaystyle-2\eta\left(Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\beta_{\textup{N}},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{U},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{V},1}^{(j)}\right),

a first-stage upper bound of
\mathrm{dist}^{(j+1)}
with respect to
\mathrm{dist}^{(j)}
,
Q_{\cdot,1}^{(j)}
, and
Q_{\cdot,2}^{(j)}
terms.

In
Steps 4
, we give further the lower bounds of
Q_{\cdot,1}^{(j)}
terms, and the upper bounds of
Q_{\cdot,2}^{(j)}
terms. After all these steps, we derive a final upper bound of
\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j+1)}
in (
B.55
). In addition to
\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}
, it is related to
\xi
and
\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}
.

In
Steps 5
, we find a suitable value for
\eta
such that the coefficient of
\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}
is less than
1
, i.e.,

\begin{split}1-2\eta&\Bigg[\frac{\alpha}{4(\sqrt{2}+1)^{2}}-2a_{2}\phi^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)\\
&-\frac{1}{4}(2+B)^{2}\alpha C_{D}-\frac{a_{1}}{2}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\right)\Bigg]\leq 1,\end{split}

(B.6)

and the coefficient of
\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}
is non-positive, i.e.,

8\eta+\frac{8\eta}{(1-B)^{2}}+\frac{4}{a_{2}}\phi\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)-\frac{1}{4\beta}\leq 0.

(B.7)

After that, we construct a recursive relationship (
B.60
) where the computational error decays. Then, provided that the recursive relationship holds for every
j
, we obtain the upper bound of estimation errors, in terms of
\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}
and
\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}
, as a combination of computational errors and statistical errors.

In
Steps 6
, we verify that the conditions stated in
Steps 2
hold recursively and conclude the proof by induction.

Step 2.
(Notations and conditions)

We begin by introducing some notations and conditions for the convergence analysis. Other notations not mentioned in this appendix are inherited from the main article. Throughout this article, the true values of all parameters are defined by letters with an asterisk superscript, i.e.,
\{\beta_{\textup{A}}^{*},\beta_{\textup{N}}^{*},\mathbf{U}^{*},\mathbf{V}^{*}\}
.

For the
j
-th iterate, we quantify the combined estimation errors up to optimal transformations as

\displaystyle\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}=\inf_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{Q}\in\mathrm{GL}(r),\\
c_{1},c_{2}\neq 0\end{subarray}}\biggl\{

\displaystyle\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{A}}^{(j)}-\beta_{\textup{A}}^{*}\right)^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(B.8)

\displaystyle+\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{N}}^{(j)}-\beta_{\textup{N}}^{*}\right)^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{W}_{N}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|(c_{1}\mathbf{U}^{(j)}\mathbf{Q}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|(c_{2}\mathbf{V}^{(j)}\mathbf{Q}^{-\top}-\mathbf{V}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\biggr\},

and the corresponding optimal alignment as
c_{1}^{(j)}
,
c_{2}^{(j)}
, and
\mathbf{Q}_{(j)}
. For notational convenience, denote
\mathbf{U}:=c_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{U}^{(j)}\mathbf{Q}_{(j)}
,
\mathbf{V}:=c_{2}^{(j)}\mathbf{V}^{(j)}\mathbf{Q}^{-\top}_{(j)}
,
\beta_{\textup{A}}:=(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{A}}^{(j)}
,
\beta_{\textup{N}}:=(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{N}}^{(j)}
,
\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}:=\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}^{*}
,
\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}:=\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{V}^{*}
,
\delta_{\beta_{\textup{A}}}:=\beta_{\textup{A}}-\beta_{\textup{A}}^{*}
, and
\delta_{\beta_{\textup{N}}}:=\beta_{\textup{N}}-\beta_{\textup{N}}^{*}
.

Suppose that the loss function satisfies RSC and RSS conditions, i.e., there exist constants
\alpha
and
\beta
with
\alpha\leq\beta
, such that

\begin{split}&\mathrm{(RSC)}\quad\frac{\alpha}{2}\left\|\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{A}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A})-\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})-\left\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}),\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{A}^{*}\right\rangle,\\
&\mathrm{(RSS)}\quad\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A})-\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})-\left\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}),\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{A}^{*}\right\rangle\leq\frac{\beta}{2}\left\|\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{A}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.\end{split}

(B.9)

As in
Nesterov, 2018
, these two conditions show jointly that

\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)-\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A})\geq\left\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}),\mathbf{A}^{*}-\mathbf{A}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2\beta}\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

(B.10)

Combining this inequality and the RSC condition, we have that

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right),\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{A}^{*}\right\rangle\geq\frac{\alpha}{2}\left\|\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{A}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2\beta}\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2},

(B.11)

which is equivalent to the restricted correlated gradient (RCG) condition in
Han et al., 2022
.

Additionally, the deviation bound is defined as

\xi:=\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{A}\in\mathcal{V}(r,D;N)\end{subarray}}\left\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}),\ \mathbf{A}\right\rangle.

(B.12)

where the model space
\mathcal{V}(r,D;N)
is defined as

\begin{split}\mathcal{V}(r,D;N):=\Big\{\mathbf{A}\in\mathbb{R}^{ND\times ND}\ \bigg|\ &\mathbf{A}=(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{V}^{\top})\otimes(\beta_{\textup{A}}\mathbf{I}_{N}+\beta_{\textup{N}}\mathbf{W}_{N}),\\
&\mathbf{U},\mathbf{V}\in\mathbb{R}^{D\times r},\ \beta_{\textup{A}},\beta_{\textup{N}}\in\mathbb{R},\ \left\|\mathbf{A}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}=1\Big\}.\end{split}

(B.13)

Let
\phi:=\left\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}=\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}
. Then, by the sub-multiplicative property of the Frobenius norm, for any
j=0,1,2,...
, we assume that the following four conditions hold:

\begin{gathered}\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq(1+c_{a})\phi,\quad\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq(1+c_{a})\phi,\\
(1+c_{a})^{-1}\phi^{-1}\leq\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{-1}\leq(1-c_{a})^{-1}\phi^{-1},\\
\text{and }(1+c_{a})^{-1}\phi^{-1}\leq\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{-1}\leq(1-c_{a})^{-1}\phi^{-1},\end{gathered}

(B.14)

where
c_{a}
is a constant. We assume that
c_{a}\leq 0.04
, where
0.04
reflects the accuracy of the initial point and can be replaced by any small positive numbers.

In addition, we assume that there exist small constants
C_{D}
and
C
related to the accuracy of the initial values, such that

\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}\leq\min\left\{\frac{C_{D}\alpha\phi^{4}}{\beta},\frac{C\alpha\phi^{2}\sigma_{r}^{2}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})}{\beta}\right\}=\frac{C\alpha\phi^{2}\sigma_{r}^{2}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})}{\beta},\quad\forall j=0,1,2,\dots,

(B.15)

which implies that

\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}\leq C_{D}\phi^{4},

(B.16)

and

\left[\|(c_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{U}^{(j)}\mathbf{Q}_{(j)}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\|(c_{2}^{(j)}\mathbf{V}^{(j)}\mathbf{Q}_{(j)}^{-\top}-\mathbf{V}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right]^{1/2}\leq C\alpha^{1/2}\beta^{-1/2}\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}).

(B.17)

Furthermore, as
\left\|\mathbf{A}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{B})\leq\left\|\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}
, we have

\left[\|(c_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{U}^{(j)}\mathbf{Q}_{(j)}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*-1/2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\|(c_{2}^{(j)}\mathbf{V}^{(j)}\mathbf{Q}_{(j)}^{-\top}-\mathbf{V}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*-1/2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right]^{1/2}\leq C\alpha^{1/2}\beta^{-1/2}.

(B.18)

Hence, we have

\begin{split}&\text{max}\left\{\|(c_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{U}^{(j)}\mathbf{Q}_{(j)}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*-1/2}\|_{\mathrm{op}},\|(c_{2}^{(j)}\mathbf{V}^{(j)}\mathbf{Q}^{-\top}_{(j)}-\mathbf{V}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*-1/2}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\right\}\\
&\leq C\alpha^{1/2}\beta^{-1/2}:=B<1.\end{split}

(B.19)

We will verify the conditions in (
B.14
) and (
B.15
) recursively in
Steps 6
.

Step 3.
(Upper bound of
\mathrm{dist}_{(j+1)}^{2}-\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}
)

By the optimality of the alignments
c_{1}^{(j+1)}
,
c_{2}^{(j+1)}
, and
\mathbf{Q}_{(j+1)}
,

\begin{split}&\mathrm{dist}_{(j+1)}^{2}\\
=&\Big\{\left((c_{1}^{(j+1)}c_{2}^{(j+1)})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{A}}^{(j+1)}-\beta_{\textup{A}}^{*}\right)^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
&~+\left((c_{1}^{(j+1)}c_{2}^{(j+1)})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{N}}^{(j+1)}-\beta_{\textup{N}}^{*}\right)^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{W}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
&~+\|(c_{1}^{(j+1)}\mathbf{U}^{(j+1)}\mathbf{Q}_{(j+1)}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
&~+\|(c_{2}^{(j+1)}\mathbf{V}^{(j+1)}\mathbf{Q}^{-\top}_{(j+1)}-\mathbf{V}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\Big\}\\
\leq&\Big\{\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{A}}^{(j+1)}-\beta_{\textup{A}}^{*}\right)^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
&~+\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{N}}^{(j+1)}-\beta_{\textup{N}}^{*}\right)^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{W}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
&~+\|(c_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{U}^{(j+1)}\mathbf{Q}_{(j)}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
&~+\|(c_{2}^{(j)}\mathbf{V}^{(j+1)}\mathbf{Q}^{-\top}_{(j)}-\mathbf{V}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\Big\}.\end{split}

(B.20)

In the following substeps, we derive upper bounds for the error of
\beta_{\textup{A}}
,
\beta_{\textup{N}}
,
\mathbf{U}
, and
\mathbf{V}
separately, and combine them to obtain a first-stage upper bound in (
B.41
).

Step 3.1
(Upper bounds of errors with respect to
\mathbf{U}
and
\mathbf{V}
)

By the gradient update rules in Algorithm
1
, we have

\displaystyle\|(c_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{U}^{(j+1)}\mathbf{Q}_{(j)}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}

(B.21)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big\|(c_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{U}^{(j)}\mathbf{Q}_{(j)}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}

\displaystyle-\eta\Big(c_{1}^{(j)}\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-2}\cdot\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{2}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\mathbf{V}^{(j)}(\mathbf{V}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{V}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{Q}_{(j)}\Big)\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\Big\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}c_{1}^{(j)2}c_{2}^{(j)2}\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-4}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{2}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\Big\|_{\text{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle-2\eta c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)}\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\Big\langle\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2},\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{2}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\Big\rangle

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}I_{\mathbf{U},2}^{(j)}-2\eta I_{\mathbf{U},1}^{(j)},

where the second equality stems from the fact
\mathbf{V}^{(j)}(\mathbf{V}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{V}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{Q}_{(j)}=c_{2}^{(j)}\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}.

For
I_{\mathbf{U},2}^{(j)}
(the second term in the RHS of (
B.21
)), by Cauchy’s inequality,

\displaystyle I_{\mathbf{U},2}^{(j)}=

\displaystyle c_{1}^{(j)2}c_{2}^{(j)2}\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-4}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{2}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\Big\|_{\text{F}}^{2}

(B.22)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c_{1}^{(j)2}c_{2}^{(j)2}\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-4}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\text{mat}(\mathcal{P}(\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}))\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}))\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\Big\|_{\text{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2c_{1}^{(j)2}c_{2}^{(j)2}\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-4}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\text{mat}(\mathcal{P}(\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}))\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}))\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\Big\|_{\text{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+2c_{1}^{(j)2}c_{2}^{(j)2}\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-4}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot

\displaystyle\Big\|\text{mat}(\mathcal{P}(\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}))\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}))\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\Big\|_{\text{F}}^{2}.

By duality of the Frobenius norm, definition of
\xi
, and Lemma
B.1
, the first term in the RHS of
I_{\mathbf{U},2}^{(j)}
can bounded by

\begin{split}&\Big\|\text{mat}(\mathcal{P}(\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}))\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}))\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\Big\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
=&\sup_{\|\mathbf{W}\|_{\text{F}}=1}\left\langle\text{mat}(\mathcal{P}(\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}))\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}))\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2},\mathbf{W}\right\rangle^{2}\\
=&\sup_{\|\mathbf{W}\|_{\text{F}}=1}\left\langle\text{mat}(\mathcal{P}(\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}))\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)})),\mathbf{W}\left(\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right)^{\top}\right\rangle^{2}\\
=&\sup_{\|\mathbf{W}\|_{\text{F}}=1}\left\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}),\mathbf{W}\left(\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right)^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\rangle^{2}\\
\leq&\|\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\text{op}}^{2}\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\|^{2}_{\text{F}}\cdot\xi^{2}\\
\leq&(1-B)^{-2}\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\|^{2}_{\text{F}}\cdot\xi^{2},\end{split}

(B.23)

where
B
is error bound defined in (
B.19
).
Thus, we have

\begin{split}I_{\mathbf{U},2}^{(j)}\leq&2(1-B)^{-2}c_{1}^{(j)2}c_{2}^{(j)2}\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-4}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\left(\xi^{2}+\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\\
=&2(1-B)^{-2}\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\left(\xi^{2}+\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\\
:=&Q_{\mathbf{U},2}^{(j)}.\end{split}

(B.24)

For
I_{\mathbf{U},1}^{(j)}
(the third term in (
B.21
)),

I_{\mathbf{U},1}^{(j)}=c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)}\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\Big\langle\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2},\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{2}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\Big\rangle.

(B.25)

For the inner product term in
I_{\mathbf{U},1}^{(j)}
, we rewrite

\begin{split}&\Big\langle\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2},\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{2}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\Big\rangle\\
=&\Big\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{2}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}),\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{V}^{\top}\Big\rangle\\
=&\Big\langle\text{mat}\left(\mathcal{P}(\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}))\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)})\right),\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{V}^{\top}\Big\rangle\\
=&\left\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}),\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{V}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\rangle\\
=&\left\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}),\left(\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{V}^{\top}-\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{V}^{*\top}-\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\right)\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\rangle\\
&+\left\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}),\left(\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{V}^{*\top}+\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\right)\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\rangle\\
:=&G^{(j)}_{1}+G^{(j)}_{2}.\end{split}

(B.26)

We keep the second term
G^{(j)}_{2}
fixed, and for the first term
G^{(j)}_{1}
, we have

\begin{split}&\left|G^{(j)}_{1}\right|\\
=&\left|\left\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}),\left(\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{V}^{\top}-\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{V}^{*\top}-\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\right)\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\rangle\right|\\
\leq&\left|\left\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}),\left(\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{V}^{\top}-\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{V}^{*\top}-\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\right)\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\rangle\right|\\
&+\left|\left\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}),\left(\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{V}^{\top}-\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{V}^{*\top}-\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\right)\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\rangle\right|.\end{split}

(B.27)

By definition of
\xi
, Cauchy’s inequality, and Lemma
B.1
, the first term in the RHS of (
B.27
) can be further bounded by

\begin{split}&\left|\left\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}),\left(\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{V}^{\top}-\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{V}^{*\top}-\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\right)\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\rangle\right|\\
\leq&\left\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\cdot\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{V}^{\top}-\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{V}^{*\top}-\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\cdot\xi\\
\leq&\left\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\cdot\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\cdot\left(\left\|\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}-\mathbf{R}^{*}\right\|_{\text{op}}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*-1/2}\right\|_{\text{op}}\right)\cdot\xi\\
\leq&\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)\cdot\left\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\cdot\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\cdot\xi,\end{split}

(B.28)

where the second inequality stems from
\mathbf{V}^{*}=\mathbf{R}^{*}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}
.
Thus, we have

\left|G^{(j)}_{1}\right|\leq\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)\cdot\left\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\cdot\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\cdot\left(\xi+\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right).

(B.29)

Therefore, we can derive the lower bound of
I_{\mathbf{U},1}^{(j)}
:

\begin{split}I_{\mathbf{U},1}^{(j)}=&~c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)}\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\Big\langle\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2},\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{2}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\Big\rangle\\
\geq&~c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)}\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\bigg[\left\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}),\left(\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{V}^{*\top}+\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}/2\right)\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\rangle\\
&-\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)\cdot\left\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\cdot\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\cdot\left(\xi+\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right)\bigg]\\
=&\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\left\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}),\left(\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{V}^{*\top}+\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}/2\right)\otimes(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\rangle\\
&-\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)\cdot\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{-1}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\\
&~~\cdot\left(\xi+\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right)\\
:=&Q_{\mathbf{U},1}^{(j)}.\end{split}

(B.30)

Combining the bound for the
I_{\mathbf{U},2}
in (
B.24
), we have

\begin{split}&\|(c_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{U}^{(j+1)}\mathbf{Q}_{(j)}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}-\|(c_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{U}^{(j)}\mathbf{Q}_{(j)}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
&\leq-2\eta Q_{\mathbf{U},1}^{(j)}+\eta^{2}Q_{\mathbf{U},2}^{(j)},\end{split}

(B.31)

where
Q_{\mathbf{U},1}^{(j)}
and
Q_{\mathbf{U},2}^{(j)}
are defined in (
B.30
) and (
B.24
), respectively.

Similarly, for
\mathbf{V}^{(j+1)}
, we can also define the quantities and show that

\begin{split}&\|(c_{2}^{(j)}\mathbf{V}^{(j+1)}\mathbf{Q}^{-\top}_{(j)}-\mathbf{V}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}-\|(c_{2}^{(j)}\mathbf{V}^{(j)}\mathbf{Q}^{-\top}_{(j)}-\mathbf{V}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
&\leq-2\eta Q_{\mathbf{V},1}^{(j)}+\eta^{2}Q_{\mathbf{V},2}^{(j)}.\end{split}

(B.32)

Step 3.2
(Upper bounds of errors with respect to
\beta_{\textup{A}}
and
\beta_{\textup{N}}
)

By the update rules in Algorithm
1
, we have

\displaystyle\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{A}}^{(j+1)}-\beta_{\textup{A}}^{*}\right)^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}

(B.33)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{A}}^{(j)}-\beta_{\textup{A}}^{*}-\eta\big((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\cdot(\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-2}\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{-2})\cdot\left\langle\mathbf{I}_{N},\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\right\rangle\big)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\cdot\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{\delta}^{2}_{\beta_{\textup{A}}}\cdot\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}\cdot(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-4}\cdot\left\langle\mathbf{I}_{N},\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\right\rangle^{2}

\displaystyle-2\eta\cdot(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-2}\cdot\mathbf{\delta}_{\beta_{\textup{A}}}\cdot\left\langle\mathbf{I}_{N},\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\right\rangle

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\mathbf{\delta}^{2}_{\beta_{\textup{A}}}\cdot\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}I_{\beta_{\textup{A}},2}^{(j)}-2\eta I_{\beta_{\textup{A}},1}^{(j)}.

For the second term in the RHS of (
B.33
),

I_{\beta_{\textup{A}},2}^{(j)}=(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-4}\cdot\left\langle\mathbf{I}_{N},\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\right\rangle^{2},

(B.34)

where
\left\langle\mathbf{I}_{N},\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\right\rangle
can be rewritten as

\begin{split}&\left\langle\mathbf{I}_{N},\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\right\rangle\\
=&\left\langle\mathbf{I}_{N},\text{mat}\left(\mathcal{P}(\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}))^{\top}\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)})\right)\right\rangle\\
=&\Big\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}),\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{N}\Big\rangle\\
=&\Big\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}),\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{N}\Big\rangle+\Big\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}),\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{N}\Big\rangle.\end{split}

(B.35)

By Cauchy’s inequality and the definition of
\xi
, we have

\begin{split}I_{\beta_{\textup{A}},2}^{(j)}=&(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-4}\cdot\left\langle\mathbf{I}_{N},\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\right\rangle^{2}\\
\leq&(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-4}\Big(2\Big\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}),\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{N}\Big\rangle^{2}\\
&+2\Big\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}),\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{N}\Big\rangle^{2}\Big)\\
\leq&(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-4}\cdot\Big(2\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\left\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
&\cdot\left(\xi^{2}+\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\Big)\\
=&2\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{-2}\cdot\left(\xi^{2}+\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\\
:=&Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},2}^{(j)}.\end{split}

(B.36)

For
I_{\beta_{\textup{A}},1}^{(j)}
(the third term in (
B.33
)), from (
B.35
), we have

\left\langle\mathbf{I}_{N},\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\right\rangle\\
=\Big\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}),\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{N}\Big\rangle.

(B.37)

Then, we have

\begin{split}I_{\beta_{\textup{A}},1}^{(j)}=&(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-2}\cdot\mathbf{\delta}_{\beta_{\textup{A}}}\cdot\left\langle\mathbf{I}_{N},\nabla_{\mathbf{B}_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\right\rangle\\
=&(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-2}\cdot\mathbf{\delta}_{\beta_{\textup{A}}}\cdot\Big\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}),\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{N}\Big\rangle\\
=&\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\mathbf{\delta}_{\beta_{\textup{A}}}\cdot\Big\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}),(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{N}\Big\rangle\\
:=&Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},1}^{(j)}.\end{split}

(B.38)

Combining the bound for the
I_{\beta_{\textup{A}},2}^{(j)}
in (
B.36
), we have

\begin{split}&\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{A}}^{(j+1)}-\beta_{\textup{A}}^{*}\right)^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}-\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{A}}^{(j)}-\beta_{\textup{A}}^{*}\right)^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
&\leq-2\eta Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},1}^{(j)}+\eta^{2}Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},2}^{(j)}.\end{split}

(B.39)

where
Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},1}^{(j)}
and
Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},2}^{(j)}
are defined in (
B.38
) and (
B.36
), respectively.

Accordingly, we also have

\begin{split}&\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{N}}^{(j+1)}-\beta_{\textup{N}}^{*}\right)^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{W}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}-\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{N}}^{(j)}-\beta_{\textup{N}}^{*}\right)^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{W}_{N}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
&\leq-2\eta Q_{\beta_{\textup{N}},1}^{(j)}+\eta^{2}Q_{\beta_{\textup{N}},2}^{(j)}.\end{split}

(B.40)

Combining the inequalities in (
B.31
), (
B.32
), (
B.39
), and (
B.40
), according to (
B.20
), we obtain the following upper bound of
\mathrm{dist}_{(j+1)}^{2}
:

\displaystyle\mathrm{dist}_{(j+1)}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}+\eta^{2}\left(Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},2}^{(j)}+Q_{\beta_{\textup{N}},2}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{U},2}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{V},2}^{(j)}\right)

(B.41)

\displaystyle-2\eta\left(Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\beta_{\textup{N}},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{U},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{V},1}^{(j)}\right).

Step 4.
(Combined upper bound of
\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j+1)}
)

In this step, we derive lower bounds of the sum of
Q_{\cdot,1}^{(j)}
, and upper bounds of the sum of
Q_{\cdot,2}^{(j)}
. Finally we obtain an upper bound as in (
B.55
). In
Step 4.1
, we find the lower bound of
Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\beta_{\textup{N}},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{U},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{V},1}^{(j)}
. In
Step 4.2
, we find the upper bound of
Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},2}^{(j)}+Q_{\beta_{\textup{N}},2}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{U},2}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{V},2}^{(j)}
.

Step 4.1
(Lower bound of
Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\beta_{\textup{N}},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{U},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{V},1}^{(j)}
)

According to (
B.38
), (
B.30
), and its analogue for
Q_{\beta_{\textup{N}},1}^{(j)}
and
Q_{\mathbf{V},1}^{(j)}
, we have

\displaystyle Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\beta_{\textup{N}},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{U},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{V},1}^{(j)}

(B.42)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2})\cdot\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{-1}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\left(\xi+\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right)+P^{(j)},

where

\displaystyle P^{(j)}:=

\displaystyle\left\langle(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\left(\mathbf{\delta}_{\beta_{\textup{A}}}\mathbf{I}_{N}+\mathbf{\delta}_{\beta_{\textup{N}}}\mathbf{W}_{N}\right),\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right\rangle

(B.43)

\displaystyle+\left\langle\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{-2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\left(\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{V}^{*\top}+\mathbf{U}^{*}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\otimes(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)},\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right\rangle.

We denote
\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(j)}
as the first order perturbation of
\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}
from the true value:

\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(j)}:=\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\otimes\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right)+\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\right)\otimes\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right).

(B.44)

By Lemma
B.2
,
\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(j)}
can be expressed as

\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(j)}=\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}+\mathbf{H}^{(j)},

(B.45)

where
\mathbf{H}^{(j)}
represents the higher-order perturbation terms in
\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}
, satisfying
\left\|\mathbf{H}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\sqrt{2}/{2}\cdot(2+B)\phi^{-2}\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}
, and
B
is error bound defined in (
B.19
).

Hence, with conditions (
B.14
) and RCG condition (
B.11
), we have

\begin{split}P^{(j)}\geq&~\frac{1}{2}\left\langle(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\left(\mathbf{\delta}_{\beta_{\textup{A}}}\mathbf{I}_{N}+\mathbf{\delta}_{\beta_{\textup{N}}}\mathbf{W}_{N}\right),\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right\rangle\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\left(\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{V}^{*\top}+\mathbf{U}^{*}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\right)\otimes(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)},\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right\rangle\\
=&~\frac{1}{2}\left\langle(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right),\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right\rangle\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\otimes(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)},\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right\rangle\\
=&~\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}+\mathbf{H}^{(j)},\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right\rangle\\
=&~\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\left\langle\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*},\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right\rangle\\
&~~~+\left\langle\mathbf{H}^{(j)},\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right\rangle+\left\langle\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(j)},\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right\rangle\bigg]\\
\geq&\frac{\alpha}{4}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\text{F}}^{2}+\frac{1}{4\beta}\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{H}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\left|\left\langle(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right),\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right\rangle\right|\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\left|\left\langle\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\otimes(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)},\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right\rangle\right|.\end{split}

(B.46)

For the third term in (
B.46
), by condition (
B.15
), we have
\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}\leq C_{D}\alpha\beta^{-1}\phi^{4}
and

\begin{split}&\left\|\mathbf{H}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\\
\leq&\frac{1}{4\beta}\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\beta\left\|\mathbf{H}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
\leq&\frac{1}{4\beta}\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\beta\cdot\left(\frac{1}{2}(2+B)^{2}\phi^{-4}\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}\right)\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}\\
\leq&\frac{1}{4\beta}\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}(2+B)^{2}\alpha C_{D}\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}.\end{split}

(B.47)

Then, for the fourth term in (
B.46
), by Lemma
B.2
and condition (
B.14
), we have

\begin{split}&\left|\left\langle(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right),\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right\rangle\right|\\
\leq&\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\xi\\
\leq&2\phi\cdot\phi^{-1}\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}\xi=2\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}\xi\\
\leq&a_{1}\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}+\frac{1}{a_{1}}\xi^{2},\end{split}

(B.48)

where the last inequality comes from the fact that
x^{2}+y^{2}\geq 2xy
, and
a_{1}
can be any positive constant.

Applying the same analysis as the fourth term in (
B.46
) on the last term in (
B.46
), it can be upper bounded as:

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\otimes(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)},\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right\rangle\right|

(B.49)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\xi

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\phi\cdot\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\phi^{-1}\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}\xi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{2}(2+B)\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}\xi

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\left(a_{1}\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}+\frac{1}{a_{1}}\xi^{2}\right).

Consequently, putting the bounds together, we obtain the lower bound of
P^{(j)}
defined in (
B.43
):

\begin{split}P^{(j)}\geq&\frac{\alpha}{4}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{1}{4\beta}\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{1}{4\beta}\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}(2+B)^{2}\alpha C_{D}\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}\right)\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\right)\left(a_{1}\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}+\frac{1}{a_{1}}\xi^{2}\right)\\
=&\frac{\alpha}{4}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{1}{8\beta}\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
&-\frac{1}{4}(2+B)^{2}\alpha C_{D}\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\right)\left(a_{1}\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}+\frac{1}{a_{1}}\xi^{2}\right).\end{split}

(B.50)

For the first term of the RHS in (
B.42
), according to the inequality
x^{2}+y^{2}\geq 2xy
, we have

\begin{split}&\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)\cdot\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{-1}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\left(\xi+\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right)\\
&\cdot\left(\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right)\\
\leq&\phi\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)\bigg[\frac{1}{a_{2}}\left(\xi+\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right)^{2}+a_{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right)^{2}\bigg]\\
\leq&2\phi\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)\bigg[\frac{1}{a_{2}}\left(\xi^{2}+\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)+a_{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\bigg]\\
\leq&\frac{2}{a_{2}}\phi\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)\left(\xi^{2}+\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)+2a_{2}\phi^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2},\end{split}

(B.51)

where
a_{2}
is a positive constant.

Furthermore, combining (
B.50
) and (
B.51
), we have

\begin{split}&Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\beta_{\textup{N}},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{U},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{V},1}^{(j)}\\
\geq&-\frac{2}{a_{2}}\phi\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)\left(\xi^{2}+\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)-2a_{2}\phi^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}\\
&+\frac{\alpha}{4}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{1}{8\beta}\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
&-\frac{1}{4}(2+B)^{2}\alpha C_{D}\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\right)\left(a_{1}\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}+\frac{1}{a_{1}}\xi^{2}\right)\\
=&\frac{\alpha}{4}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
&+\bigg[\frac{1}{8\beta}-\frac{2}{a_{2}}\phi\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)\bigg]\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
&-\bigg[\frac{2}{a_{2}}\phi\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)+\frac{1}{2a_{1}}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\right)\bigg]\xi^{2}\\
&-\bigg[2a_{2}\phi^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)+\frac{1}{4}(2+B)^{2}\alpha C_{D}+\frac{a_{1}}{2}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\right)\bigg]\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}.\end{split}

(B.52)

Finally, by Lemma
B.4
, we derive the following lower bound of (
B.42
)

\begin{split}&Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\beta_{\textup{N}},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{U},1}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{V},1}^{(j)}\\
\geq&\Bigg[\frac{\alpha}{4(\sqrt{2}+1)^{2}}-2a_{2}\phi^{-1}(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2})-\frac{1}{4}(2+B)^{2}\alpha C_{D}-\frac{a_{1}}{2}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\right)\Bigg]\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}\\
&+\bigg[\frac{1}{8\beta}-\frac{2}{a_{2}}\phi(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2})\bigg]\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
&-\bigg[\frac{2}{a_{2}}\phi(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2})+\frac{1}{2a_{1}}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\right)\bigg]\xi^{2}.\end{split}

(B.53)

Step 4.2.
(Upper bound of
Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},2}^{(j)}+Q_{\beta_{\textup{N}},2}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{U},2}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{V},2}^{(j)}
)

Following (
B.36
), (
B.24
), and its analogue for
Q_{\beta_{\textup{N}},2}^{(j)}
and
Q_{\mathbf{V},2}^{(j)}
, together with condition (
B.14
), we have

\begin{split}&Q_{\beta_{\textup{A}},2}^{(j)}+Q_{\beta_{\textup{N}},2}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{U},2}^{(j)}+Q_{\mathbf{V},2}^{(j)}\\
=&4\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{-2}\cdot\left(\xi^{2}+\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\\
&+4(1-B)^{-2}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{-2}\cdot\left(\xi^{2}+\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\\
\leq&\left(8+8(1-B)^{-2}\right)\left(\xi^{2}+\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right).\end{split}

(B.54)

So far, we have derived the bounds of all parts of (
B.41
), namely the lower bounds in (
B.52
), and upper bounds in (
B.54
). Combining them, we have

\begin{split}&\mathrm{dist}_{(j+1)}^{2}\\
\leq&\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}+\eta^{2}\left(8+8(1-B)^{-2}\right)\left(\xi^{2}+\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\\
&-2\eta\Bigg\{\Bigg[\frac{\alpha}{4(\sqrt{2}+1)^{2}}-2a_{2}\phi^{-1}(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2})-\frac{1}{4}(2+B)^{2}\alpha C_{D}-\frac{a_{1}}{2}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\right)\Bigg]\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}\\
&+\bigg[\frac{1}{8\beta}-\frac{2}{a_{2}}\phi\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)\bigg]\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
&-\bigg[\frac{2}{a_{2}}\phi\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)+\frac{1}{2a_{1}}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\right)\bigg]\xi^{2}\Bigg\}\\
=&\left\{1-2\eta\Bigg[\frac{\alpha}{4(\sqrt{2}+1)^{2}}-2a_{2}\phi^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)-\frac{1}{4}(2+B)^{2}\alpha C_{D}\right.\\
&\qquad\left.-\frac{a_{1}}{2}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\right)\Bigg]\right\}\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}\\
&+\eta\bigg[8\eta+\frac{8\eta}{(1-B)^{2}}+\frac{4}{a_{2}}\phi\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)-\frac{1}{4\beta}\bigg]\left\|\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
&+\eta\bigg[8\eta+\frac{8\eta}{(1-B)^{2}}+\frac{4}{a_{2}}\phi\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)+\frac{1}{a_{1}}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\right)\bigg]\xi^{2}.\end{split}

(B.55)

Step 5.
(Recursive relationship between
\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}
and
\mathrm{dist}_{(j+1)}^{2}
)

As in (
B.19
),
B=C\alpha^{1/2}\beta^{-1/2}\leq C
. For the undefined positive coefficients
\eta
,
a_{1}
, and
a_{2}
, let

\eta=\frac{\eta_{0}}{\beta},\quad a_{1}=\alpha C_{D},\quad\text{and}\quad a_{2}=\alpha^{1/2}\beta^{1/2}\phi.

(B.56)

For the coefficient of the second term in the RHS of (
B.55
),

\begin{split}&8\eta+\frac{8\eta}{(1-B)^{2}}+\frac{4}{a_{2}}\phi(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2})-\frac{1}{4\beta}\\
=&\frac{\eta_{0}}{\beta}\left(8+\frac{8}{(1-B)^{2}}\right)+\frac{4C}{\beta}\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}}{1-B}+\frac{1}{2}\right)-\frac{1}{4\beta}\\
\leq&\frac{1}{\beta}\left(\frac{24}{100}+14C-\frac{1}{4}\right)\\
\leq&0.\end{split}

(B.57)

Form now on, for the sake of simplicity,
C
is denoted as a constant whose exact value may change in different contexts. For the coefficient of the third term of (
B.55
),

\begin{split}&\eta\bigg[8\eta+\frac{8\eta}{(1-B)^{2}}+\frac{4}{a_{2}}\phi(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2})+\frac{1}{a_{1}}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\right)\bigg]\\
\leq&\frac{\eta_{0}}{\beta}\bigg[\frac{1}{4\beta}+\frac{1}{a_{1}}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\right)\bigg]\\
\leq&\frac{\eta_{0}}{\beta}\bigg[\frac{1}{4\alpha}+\frac{1}{\alpha C_{D}}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+C)\right)\bigg]\\
=&\frac{\eta_{0}}{\alpha\beta}\bigg[\frac{1}{4}+\frac{1}{C_{D}}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+C)\right)\bigg]\\
:=&C\alpha^{-1}\beta^{-1}\eta_{0}.\end{split}

(B.58)

For the coefficient of
-2\eta
in the first term in the RHS of (
B.55
), we derive a lower bound by

\begin{split}&\frac{\alpha}{4(\sqrt{2}+1)^{2}}-2a_{2}\phi^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}B}{1-B}+\frac{B}{2}\right)-\frac{1}{4}(2+B)^{2}\alpha C_{D}-\frac{a_{1}}{2}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\right)\\
=&\alpha\bigg[\frac{1}{4(\sqrt{2}+1)^{2}}-2C\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}}{1-B}+\frac{1}{2}\right)-\frac{1}{4}(2+B)^{2}C_{D}-\frac{C_{D}}{2}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\right)\bigg]\\
\geq&\alpha\bigg[\frac{1}{4(\sqrt{2}+1)^{2}}-2C\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}}{1-C}+\frac{1}{2}\right)-\frac{1}{4}(2+C)^{2}C_{D}-\frac{C_{D}}{2}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+C)\right)\bigg]\\
:=&\alpha\cdot\frac{C}{2}.\end{split}

(B.59)

Therefore, we derive the following recursive relationship:

\mathrm{dist}_{(j+1)}^{2}\leq(1-C\eta_{0}\alpha\beta^{-1})\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}+C\eta_{0}\alpha^{-1}\beta^{-1}\xi^{2}.

(B.60)

When
C_{D},C
is small enough,
C/2
is a positive constant with
C\leq 1/2(\sqrt{2}+1)^{2}
. Then, the coefficient of
\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}
,
1-C\eta_{0}\alpha\beta^{-1}
, is smaller than 1.

In other words, the recursive relationship (
B.60
) holds for
\mathrm{dist}_{(j+1)}^{2}
. Suppose that the conditions hold for every
j\geq 0
, since
\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(1-C\eta_{0}\alpha\beta^{-1})^{j}<\infty
,

\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}\leq(1-C\eta_{0}\alpha\beta^{-1})^{j}\mathrm{dist}_{(0)}^{2}+C\eta_{0}\alpha^{-2}\xi^{2},

(B.61)

when conditions (
B.14
) and (
B.15
) of
j
-th iteration are satisfied, which are verify in the next step.

For the error bound of
\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}
, by Lemmas
B.2
and
B.4
, and conditions (
B.14
) and (
B.15
), we have

\begin{split}&\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
\leq&4\left\|\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\otimes(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
&+4\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\left((c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+2\left\|H^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
\leq&4C(\phi^{-1}\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}\cdot\phi)^{2}+2C(\phi^{-2}\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2})^{2}\\
\leq&4C\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}+2CC_{D}\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}\\
\leq&C(1-C\eta_{0}\alpha\beta^{-1})^{j}\mathrm{dist}_{(0)}^{2}+C\eta_{0}\alpha^{-2}\xi^{2}\\
\leq&C(1-C\eta_{0}\alpha\beta^{-1})^{j}\left(\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(0)}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(0)}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)+C\eta_{0}\alpha^{-2}\xi^{2}.\end{split}

(B.62)

Step 6.
(Verification of the conditions)

In this step, we verify the conditions (
B.14
) and (
B.15
) needed for the convergence analysis recursively.

For
j=0
, since the initialization condition
\mathrm{dist}_{(0)}^{2}\leq\min\left\{C_{D}\alpha\beta^{-1}\phi^{4},C\alpha\beta^{-1}\phi^{2}\sigma_{r}^{2}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})\right\}=C\alpha\beta^{-1}\phi^{2}\sigma_{r}^{2}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})
holds, we have

\begin{split}\left\|(c_{1}^{(0)}c_{2}^{(0)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(0)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}&=\left\|(c_{1}^{(0)}c_{2}^{(0)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(0)}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}+\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\\
&\leq\left\|(c_{1}^{(0)}c_{2}^{(0)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(0)}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\left\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\\
&\leq C\phi^{-1}\mathrm{dist}_{(0)}+\phi\\
&\leq\left(C\sqrt{C_{D}}+1\right)\phi\\
&=(1+c_{a})\phi,\\
\text{and }\left\|(c_{1}^{(0)}c_{2}^{(0)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(0)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{-1}&=\left\|(c_{1}^{(0)}c_{2}^{(0)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(0)}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}+\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{-1}\\
&\leq\bigg|\left\|(c_{1}^{(0)}c_{2}^{(0)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(0)}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}-\left\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\bigg|^{-1}\\
&\leq\big|C\phi^{-1}\mathrm{dist}_{(0)}-\phi\big|^{-1}\\
&\leq\left(1-C\sqrt{C_{D}}\right)^{-1}\phi^{-1}\\
&=(1-c_{a})^{-1}\phi^{-1},\end{split}

(B.63)

where
C
is a positive constant.
Therefore, we have

(1+c_{a})^{-1}\phi^{-1}\leq\left\|(c_{1}^{(j)}c_{2}^{(j)})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{-1}\leq(1-c_{a})^{-1}\phi^{-1}.

(B.64)

For
\|(c_{1}^{(0)}c_{2}^{(0)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(0)}\|_{\text{F}}
and
\|(c_{1}^{(0)}c_{2}^{(0)})\mathbf{B}_{2}^{(0)}\|_{\text{F}}^{-1}
, similar derivations as above lead to the conclusion.

Then, if conditions (
B.15
) and (
B.14
) hold at the
j
-th iterate, for the
(j+1)
-th iterate,

\begin{split}\mathrm{dist}_{(j+1)}^{2}&\leq(1-C\eta_{0}\alpha\beta^{-1})\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}+C\eta_{0}\alpha^{-2}\xi^{2}\\
&\leq\frac{C\alpha\phi^{2}\sigma_{r}^{2}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})}{\beta}-\eta_{0}\phi^{2}\sigma_{r}^{2}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})\alpha^{2}\beta^{-1}\left(\frac{C}{\beta}-\frac{C\xi^{2}\beta}{\alpha^{4}\phi^{2}\sigma_{r}^{2}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})}\right).\end{split}

(B.65)

Since
\phi^{2}\sigma_{r}^{2}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})\geq C\beta^{2}\xi^{2}\alpha^{-4}
for some universally big constant, we can verify that

\frac{C_{1}}{\beta}-\frac{C_{2}\xi^{2}\beta}{\alpha^{4}\phi^{2}\sigma_{r}^{2}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})}\geq 0,

(B.66)

and hence

\mathrm{dist}_{(j+1)}^{2}\leq\frac{C\alpha\phi^{2}\sigma_{r}^{2}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})}{\beta}.

(B.67)

By the same argument as
j=0
, we can verify that condition (
B.14
) holds for all
j>0
, and hence the induction is finished.

The update rules in Algorithm
1
require the inversion of Gram matrices
\mathbf{V}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{V}^{(j)}
and
\mathbf{U}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}^{(j)}
. We verify the validity of these updates by proving, via mathematical induction, that
\mathbf{U}^{(j)}
and
\mathbf{V}^{(j)}
maintain full column rank for all
j\geq 0
.

When
j=0
, since the initialization condition
\textup{dist}_{(0)}^{2}\leq C\alpha\beta^{-1}\phi^{2}\sigma_{r}^{2}(\mathbf{B}_{\textup{var}}^{*})
holds, by the definition of the distance metric, we have the lower bound
\textup{dist}_{(0)}^{2}\geq\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{B}_{\textup{var}}^{*})\|\mathbf{U}^{(0)}\mathbf{Q}-\mathbf{U}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}
. Combining these yields:

\|\mathbf{U}^{(0)}\mathbf{Q}-\mathbf{U}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}\leq\sqrt{C\alpha\beta^{-1}}\sqrt{\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{B}_{\textup{var}}^{*})}.

(B.68)

Using Weyl’s inequality for singular values
(
Horn and Johnson, 2012
)
, we obtain

\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{U}^{(0)})=\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{U}^{(0)}\mathbf{Q})\geq\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{U}^{*})-\|\mathbf{U}^{(0)}\mathbf{Q}-\mathbf{U}^{*}\|_{\text{F}}.

(B.69)

Since
\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{U}^{*})=\sqrt{\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{B}_{\textup{var}}^{*})}
, provided the constant
C
is sufficiently small, we have
\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{U}^{(0)})>0
. Thus,
\mathbf{U}^{(0)}
has full column rank, and
\mathbf{U}^{(0)\top}\mathbf{U}^{(0)}
is invertible. The same logic applies to
\mathbf{V}^{(0)}
.

Assume that at iteration
j
,
\mathbf{U}^{(j)}
and
\mathbf{V}^{(j)}
have full column rank, the gradient update step for
j+1
is well-defined. Since the condition (
B.15
) has been verified to hold for all
j\geq 0
, we follow the same derivation as in the
j=0
:

\|\mathbf{U}^{(j+1)}\mathbf{Q}-\mathbf{U}^{*}\|_{F}\leq\frac{\textup{dist}_{(j+1)}^{2}}{\phi\sqrt{\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{B}_{\textup{var}}^{*})}}\leq\sqrt{C\alpha\beta^{-1}}\sqrt{\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{B}_{\textup{var}}^{*})}.

(B.70)

Applying Weyl’s inequality again:

\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{U}^{(j+1)})\geq\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{U}^{*})-\|\mathbf{U}^{(j+1)}\mathbf{Q}-\mathbf{U}^{*}\|_{F}>0.

(B.71)

Therefore,
\mathbf{U}^{(j+1)}
(and similarly
\mathbf{V}^{(j+1)}
) maintains full column rank.

B.2
Auxiliary Lemmas

The first lemma states several perturbation bounds for matrix decomposition, and is similar to the Lemma 12 in
Tong et al., 2021
.

Lemma B.1
.

For any
\mathbf{U}\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times r}
,
\mathbf{V}\in\mathbb{R}^{q\times r}
, diagonal matrix
\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
, and orthonormal matrices
\mathbf{U}^{*}\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times r}
and
\mathbf{V}^{*}\in\mathbb{R}^{q\times r}
, suppose that

\max\left\{\left\|(\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{*-1/2}\right\|_{\text{op}},\left\|(\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{V}^{*})\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{*-1/2}\right\|_{\text{op}}\right\}<1.

(B.72)

Then, we have

\begin{split}\left\|\mathbf{U}(\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{U})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\text{op}}&\leq\frac{1}{1-\left\|(\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-1/2^{*}}\right\|_{\text{op}}};\\
\left\|\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\text{op}}&\leq\frac{1}{1-\left\|(\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{V}^{*})\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-1/2^{*}}\right\|_{\text{op}}};\\
\left\|\mathbf{U}(\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{U})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{*1/2}-\mathbf{L}^{*}\right\|_{\text{op}}&\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\,\left\|(\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-1/2^{*}}\right\|_{\text{op}}}{1-\left\|(\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-1/2^{*}}\right\|_{\text{op}}};\\
\text{and}\quad\left\|\mathbf{V}(\mathbf{V}^{\top}\mathbf{V})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{*1/2}-\mathbf{R}^{*}\right\|_{\text{op}}&\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\,\left\|(\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{V}^{*})\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-1/2^{*}}\right\|_{\text{op}}}{1-\left\|(\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{V}^{*})\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-1/2^{*}}\right\|_{\text{op}}}.\end{split}

(B.73)

where
\mathbf{U}^{*}=\mathbf{L}^{*}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{*1/2}
and
\mathbf{V}^{*}=\mathbf{R}^{*}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{*1/2}
.

The following lemma is a key tool in our analysis. It establishes a quantitative
relationship between the total estimation error
\mathrm{dist}^{2}
(defined in (
11
)) and the component-wise estimation errors after optimal alignment. The core result of this lemma is to show that these component-wise errors, as well as the higher-order perturbation term
\mathbf{H}
, are well-controlled and can be uniformly bounded by functions of the total distance
\mathrm{dist}^{2}
. This allows us to effectively manage and bound the error propagation in the subsequent convergence analysis.

Lemma B.2
.

Define

\begin{split}&\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}=\beta_{\textup{A}}^{*}\mathbf{I}_{N}+\beta_{\textup{N}}^{*}\mathbf{W}_{N},\quad\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}=\mathbf{U}^{*}\mathbf{V}^{*\top},\\
&\mathbf{B}_{1}=\beta_{\textup{A}}\mathbf{I}_{N}+\beta_{\textup{N}}\mathbf{W}_{N}~~\text{and}~~\mathbf{B}_{2}=\mathbf{U}\mathbf{V}^{\top},\end{split}

(B.74)

with
\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*},\mathbf{B}_{1}\in

\mathbb{R}^{N\times N}
,
\mathbf{W}_{N},\mathbf{I}_{N}\in

\mathbb{R}^{N\times N}
,
\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*},\mathbf{B}_{2}\in

\mathbb{R}^{D\times D}
and
\mathbf{W}_{N},\mathbf{I}_{N}
are defined as before.
\beta_{\textup{A}}^{*},\beta_{\textup{N}}^{*},\beta_{\textup{A}},\beta_{\textup{N}}\in\mathbb{R}
and
\left\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}=\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}=\phi
.

Meanwhile, define the errors

\begin{split}D:=&\inf_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\in\mathrm{GL}(r),\\[2.0pt]
c_{1}^{\prime},c_{2}^{\prime}\neq 0\end{subarray}}\biggl\{\left((c_{1}^{\prime}c_{2}^{\prime})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{A}}-\beta_{\textup{A}}^{*}\right)^{2}\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\left((c_{1}^{\prime}c_{2}^{\prime})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{N}}-\beta_{\textup{N}}^{*}\right)^{2}\|\mathbf{W}_{N}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\[-16.0pt]
&\qquad\qquad+\|(c_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\|(c_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{V}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime-\top}-\mathbf{V}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\biggr\}\\
:=&\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{A}}-\beta_{\textup{A}}^{*}\right)^{2}\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{N}}-\beta_{\textup{N}}^{*}\right)^{2}\|\mathbf{W}_{N}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\|(c_{1}\mathbf{U}\mathbf{Q}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\|(c_{2}\mathbf{V}\mathbf{Q}^{-\top}-\mathbf{V}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.\end{split}

Define

\begin{split}&\mathbf{\Delta}_{\beta_{\textup{A}}}=(c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{A}}-\beta_{\textup{A}}^{*},\quad\mathbf{\Delta}_{\beta_{\textup{N}}}=(c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{N}}-\beta_{\textup{N}}^{*},\\
&\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}=c_{1}\mathbf{U}\mathbf{Q}-\mathbf{U}^{*}~~\text{and}~~\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}=c_{2}\mathbf{V}\mathbf{Q}^{-\top}-\mathbf{V}^{*},\end{split}

(B.75)

and define

\begin{split}&\mathbf{H}_{1}:=\left((c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\otimes\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}\right)+\left((c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}\right)\otimes\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right),\\
&\mathbf{H}:=\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}-\mathbf{B}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}+\mathbf{H}_{1}.\end{split}

(B.76)

Assume that
D\leq\min\left\{C_{D}\phi^{4},C\phi^{2}\sigma_{r}^{2}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})\right\}=C\phi^{2}\sigma_{r}^{2}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})
, where
C_{D},C
are sufficiently small constants, then we have

\begin{split}&\left\|\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\phi^{-1}D^{1/2},\\
&\left\|\left((c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\phi^{-1}D^{1/2},\\
&\left\|\mathbf{H}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\phi^{-2}D,\\
&\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq CD.\end{split}

(B.77)

Proof of Lemma
B.2
.

We start from the decomposing the matrix
\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}
.

\begin{split}\mathbf{B}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}&=\left((c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}+\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\otimes\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}+\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right)\\
&=\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}+\left((c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}+\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right)\\
&\quad+\left((c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\otimes\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right)\\
&=\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}+\left((c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\otimes(c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}+(c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}\otimes\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right)\\
&\quad-\left((c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\otimes\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right)\\
&=\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}+\mathbf{H}_{1}-\left((c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\otimes\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right).\end{split}

(B.78)

Then

\mathbf{H}=\left((c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\otimes\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right).

(B.79)

Since
\left\langle\mathbf{I}_{N},\mathbf{W}_{N}\right\rangle=0
, we have

\begin{split}&\left\|(c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
=&\left\|\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{A}}-\beta_{\textup{A}}^{*}\right)\mathbf{I}_{N}+\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{N}}-\beta_{\textup{N}}^{*}\right)\mathbf{W}_{N}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
=&\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\beta_{\textup{A}}}\mathbf{I}_{N}+\mathbf{\Delta}_{\beta_{\textup{N}}}\mathbf{W}_{N}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
=&\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\beta_{\textup{A}}}\mathbf{I}_{N}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\beta_{\textup{N}}}\mathbf{W}_{N}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
=&\phi^{-2}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\beta_{\textup{A}}}\mathbf{I}_{N}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\beta_{\textup{N}}}\mathbf{W}_{N}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\\
\leq&\phi^{-2}D.\end{split}

(B.80)

So we can derive
\left\|(c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\phi^{-1}D^{1/2}.

Since
D\leq\min\left\{C_{D}\phi^{4},C\sigma_{r}^{2}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})\right\}=C\phi^{2}\sigma_{r}^{2}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})
,
similar to the analysis of (
B.15
), we have
D\leq C_{D}\phi^{4}
and
\text{max}\left\{\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*-1/2}\right\|_{\text{op}},\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*-1/2}\right\|_{\text{op}}\right\}\leq C^{1/2}:=B<1.

For
\left\|(c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}
, from Lemma 13 in
Tong et al., 2021
, we have

\begin{split}&\left\|(c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
\leq&\left(1+\frac{1}{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*-1/2}\right\|_{\text{op}}\vee\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*-1/2}\right\|_{\text{op}}\right)\right)^{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right)^{2}\\
\leq&2\left(1+\frac{B}{2}\right)^{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\\
=&2\left(1+\frac{B}{2}\right)^{2}\phi^{-2}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{\Delta}_{\mathbf{V}}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\\
\leq&2\left(1+\frac{B}{2}\right)^{2}\phi^{-2}D=\frac{1}{2}(2+B)^{2}\phi^{-2}D.\end{split}

(B.81)

Thus we derive
\left\|\left((c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq(\sqrt{2}/2)(2+B)\phi^{-1}D^{1/2}.

Combining the two upper bounds above, we can derive the upper bound about
\mathbf{H}
:

\begin{split}\left\|\mathbf{H}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}=&\left\|\left((c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\otimes\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\\
=&\left\|\left((c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\cdot\left\|\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\\
\leq&\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\phi^{-1}D^{1/2}\cdot\phi^{-1}D^{1/2}\\
=&\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(2+B)\phi^{-2}D.\end{split}

(B.82)

For
\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}
, from the decomposition
\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}
, we have

\begin{split}&\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
\leq&3\left\|\left((c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+3\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+3\left\|\mathbf{H}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
\leq&3\left\|\left((c_{1}c_{2})\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\left\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+3\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\left\|\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+3\left\|\mathbf{H}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
\leq&3(\phi^{-1}D^{1/2}\cdot\phi)^{2}+\frac{3}{2}(2+B)^{2}(\phi^{-1}D^{1/2}\cdot\phi)^{2}+\frac{3}{2}(2+B)^{2}(\phi^{-2}D)^{2}\\
\leq&CD+CD+C\phi^{-4}D^{2}\\
\leq&CD+CC_{D}D\\
\leq&CD.\end{split}

(B.83)

∎

The next lemma gives us a technique to analyze the upper bound of the distance between our estimation and true values. Similar results are developed as in Lemma 11 in
Tong et al., 2021
.

Lemma B.3
.

For any factor matrix,
\mathbf{F}=[\mathbf{L}^{\top}~\mathbf{R}^{\top}]^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{(n_{1}+n_{2})\times r},
the distance between
\mathbf{F}
and
\mathbf{F}^{*}
satisfies

\textup{dist}(\mathbf{F},\mathbf{F}^{*})\leq\left(\sqrt{2}+1\right)^{1/2}\left\|\mathbf{L}\mathbf{R}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}^{*}\right\|_{\textup{F}}.

(B.84)

where

\textup{dist}^{2}(\mathbf{F},\mathbf{F}^{*}):=\inf_{\mathbf{Q}\in\mathrm{GL}(r)}\left\|(\mathbf{L}\mathbf{Q}-\mathbf{L}^{*})\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\textup{F}}^{2}+\left\|(\mathbf{R}\mathbf{Q}^{-\top}-\mathbf{R}^{*})\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\textup{F}}^{2}.

(B.85)

Based on Lemma
B.3
, we can utilize the invariant metric defined in this article to obtain the following lower bound for the distance between the estimated and true values.

Lemma B.4
.

For
\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}
,
\mathbf{B}_{1}
,
\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}
,
\mathbf{B}_{2}
, and
D
defined in Lemma
B.2
, we have

\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\geq(\sqrt{2}+1)^{-2}D.

(B.86)

Proof of Lemma
B.4
.

Firstly, note that

\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}=\left\|\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{2})\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{1})^{\top}-\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*})^{\top}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

(B.87)

Apply Lamma
B.3
on
\left\|\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{2})\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{1})^{\top}-\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*})^{\top}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}
, we have

\begin{split}&\left\|\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{2})\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{1})^{\top}-\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*})^{\top}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
\geq&(\sqrt{2}+1)^{-1}\inf_{\begin{subarray}{c}c\neq 0\end{subarray}}\big\{\left\|\left(c\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{2})-\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*})\right)\left\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\[-6.0pt]
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left\|\left(c^{-1}\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{1})-\text{vec}(\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*})\right)\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\big\}\\
=&(\sqrt{2}+1)^{-1}\inf_{\begin{subarray}{c}c\neq 0\end{subarray}}\big\{\left\|c\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\left\|c^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\big\}.\end{split}

(B.88)

For
\left\|c^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}
, since
\left\langle\mathbf{I}_{N},\mathbf{W}_{N}\right\rangle=0
, we have

\left\|c^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}=\left\|\left(c^{-1}\beta_{\textup{A}}-\beta_{\textup{A}}^{*}\right)\mathbf{I}_{N}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\left\|\left(c^{-1}\beta_{\textup{N}}-\beta_{\textup{N}}^{*}\right)\mathbf{W}_{N}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

(B.89)

For
\left\|c\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}
, we apply Lamma
B.3
again,

\left\|c\mathbf{B}_{2}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\geq(\sqrt{2}+1)^{-1}\inf_{\mathbf{Q}\in\mathrm{GL}(r)}\left\|(c_{1}\mathbf{U}\mathbf{Q}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\left\|(c_{2}\mathbf{V}\mathbf{Q}^{-\top}-\mathbf{V}^{*})\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

(B.90)

where
c_{1},c_{2}\neq 0
and
c_{1}c_{2}=c.

Therefore, combine the above results, we have

\begin{split}&\left\|\mathbf{B}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
\geq&(\sqrt{2}+1)^{-2}\inf_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{Q}\in\mathrm{GL}(r),\\[2.0pt]
c_{1},c_{2}\neq 0\end{subarray}}\biggl\{\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{A}}-\beta_{\textup{A}}^{*}\right)^{2}\|\mathbf{I}_{N}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\left((c_{1}c_{2})^{-1}\beta_{\textup{N}}-\beta_{\textup{N}}^{*}\right)^{2}\|\mathbf{W}_{N}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{2}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\[-14.0pt]
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\qquad+\|(c_{1}\mathbf{U}\mathbf{Q}-\mathbf{U}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\|(c_{2}\mathbf{V}\mathbf{Q}^{-\top}-\mathbf{V}^{*})\mathbf{\Sigma}^{*1/2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{B}_{1}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\biggr\}\\
=&(\sqrt{2}+1)^{-2}D.\end{split}

∎

Appendix C
Statistical Theory

This appendix provides the complete statistical theory and non-asymptotic analysis for the proposed RRNAR estimators. Our objective is to establish the theoretical guarantees for both the lag-1 and the general lag-
L
models, culminating in the proofs of the main theorems (Theorem
2
and Corollary
1
) presented in the main article.

The appendix is organized as follows:

•

Main Results (Sections
C.1
–
C.5
):
We first develop the complete theory for the RRNAR model. This includes the proofs for the final statistical error rates (Section
C.1
and
C.2
), which are built upon three key components: the verification of RSC and RSS conditions (Section
C.3
), the high-probability bound for the statistical deviation
\xi
(Section
C.4
), and the analysis of the initialization procedure (Section
C.5
).

•

Auxiliary Lemmas (Section
C.6
):
The appendix concludes with a section containing all necessary auxiliary lemmas, including covering number arguments and matrix perturbation bounds, that support the main proofs.

C.1
Proof of Theorem
2

The proof of Theorem
2
integrates the computational convergence guarantee of Theorem
1
with the statistical properties of the model and our specific initialization procedure from Section
C.5
. The logic is as follows:

First, we invoke Lemmas
C.1
and
C.2
to establish that, with high probability, the RSC and RSS conditions hold, and the statistical deviation term is bounded by

\xi\leq M_{2}\sqrt{\mathrm{df_{NLR}}/T},

where
\mathrm{df_{NLR}}=2+r+2Dr
.
The crucial step is then to analyze the initialization procedure. We show that the theoretical target of our initialization,
\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}
, has an error of
\|\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}-\mathbf{A}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq 2\xi/\alpha
, which is on the order of
\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})
. We then argue that since this target error is so small, our practical estimator
\mathbf{A}^{(0)}
obtained after a moderate number of ALS steps, is guaranteed to satisfy condition required by Theorem
1
. Finally, we substitute these bounds into the recursive formula of Theorem
1
, and the final error
\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(J)}
is dominated by the statistical error, achieving the desired bound of
\mathcal{O}(\alpha^{-2}M_{2}^{2}\mathrm{df_{NLR}}/T)
.

The detailed proof is as follows. By Lemmas
C.1
and
C.2
, we know that with probability at least
1-C\exp\{-CDr\}
, the empirical loss function
\mathcal{L}_{T}
satisfies the RSC and RSS conditions with constants
\alpha=\alpha_{\mathrm{RSC}}
and
\beta=\beta_{\mathrm{RSS}}
, and the statistical deviation
\xi
is bounded by

\xi\leq M_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{df_{NLR}}}{T}}.

(C.1)

Next, we analyze the statistical properties of the initialization procedure from Section
C.5
. Let
\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}
denote the theoretical global solution to the structured minimization problem (
C.58
). By the optimality of
\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}
, we have
\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}})\leq\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})
. Applying the RSC condition (from Lemma
C.1
), we have:

\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\geq\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}})+\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}),\mathbf{A}^{*}-\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}\rangle+\frac{\alpha}{2}\|\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}-\mathbf{A}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

(C.2)

Combining these two inequalities yields:

0\geq\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}})-\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\geq\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}),\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}-\mathbf{A}^{*}\rangle+\frac{\alpha}{2}\|\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}-\mathbf{A}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

(C.3)

Rearranging the terms, we get:

\frac{\alpha}{2}\|\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}-\mathbf{A}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq-\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}),\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}-\mathbf{A}^{*}\rangle=\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}),\mathbf{A}^{*}-\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}\rangle.

(C.4)

Thus, by the definition of
\xi
and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality:

\langle\nabla\overline{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}),\mathbf{A}^{*}-\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}\rangle\leq\xi\cdot(\|\mathbf{A}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}).

(C.5)

Substituting this back into (
C.4
), we have:

\frac{\alpha}{2}\|\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}-\mathbf{A}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq\xi\cdot(\|\mathbf{A}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}).

(C.6)

Assuming
\|\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}-\mathbf{A}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}>0
, we can divide both sides to obtain:

\|\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}-\mathbf{A}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq 2\xi/\alpha.

(C.7)

This key result shows that the target of our initialization procedure
\mathbf{A}_{\mathrm{opt}}
, is statistically consistent and its error is controlled by
\xi
, which is of order
\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})
.
